Appendix B
Overall impact of Z3 version.

Table
4
reports the mean verification time of 35 benchmarks across three categories, each measured over 10 runs on Z3 4.14.1 and Z3 4.11.2.
Among the 32 benchmarks where both versions terminated within the time limit, all but one (Indexed-Matrix(4D)) exhibit a difference of at least
1.05\times
in total time, with speed ratios ranging from
1.1\times
to over
40\times
.
Three additional benchmarks exhibit a qualitative difference: Z3 4.11.2 fails to terminate on Swap and Trace-Matrix in the nested array category, while Z3 4.14.1 fails on Add-Array in the Tanaka et al.
Neither version is uniformly faster; in nested arrays, Z3 4.14.1 is faster on 13 benchmarks while Z3 4.11.2 is faster on 5, whereas in the Tanaka et al. Z3 4.11.2 is faster on 5 (including the timeout case) versus 3 for Z3 4.14.1.

Breakdown into ownership inference and refinement inference phases reveals an interesting pattern: in 15 out of 32 comparable benchmarks the faster version
differs
between the two phases (Table
5
, “Rev.”; Figure
11
).
For example, Init-Matrix(3D) runs
3.8\times
faster on Z3 4.14.1 during ownership (15.0 s vs. 56.9 s) yet
4.0\times
slower during refinement (187 s vs. 46.2 s), yielding a net
2.0\times
slowdown.
Lower-Triangle shows the opposite pattern: ownership is slightly faster on Z3 4.11.2 (
1.4\times
), while refinement is
23.8\times
faster on Z3 4.14.1, producing an overall
21.3\times
speedup.
An extreme case is init-1000 under the Tanaka et al. encoding, where ownership is
11.1\times
faster on Z3 4.14.1 but refinement is
703.5\times
faster on Z3 4.11.2.
These reversals indicate that the two verification phases exercise distinct constraint patterns, and that version changes in Z3’s internal heuristics can shift performance in opposite directions across these patterns.

Appendix C
Full Definition

C.1
Syntax

\begin{array}[]{llll}\tau^{-}&::=&\mathbf{int}\mid\tau^{-}\TREF\\
e&::=&x\mid\LET x=n\IN e\mid\LET x=\mathbf{null}\IN e\mid\LET x=y\mathop{-}z\IN e\\
&\mid&\IFNP x\THEN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}\ELSE e_{{\mathrm{1}}}\mid\LET x=f(y_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,y_{\mathit{n}})\IN e\mid\LET x=e_{{\mathrm{0}}}\IN e_{{\mathrm{1}}}\\
&\mid&\LET x=\ALLOC y:\tau^{-}\TREF\IN e\mid\LET x=\ast y\IN e\mid x\WRITE y\SEQ e\mid\LET x=y\boxplus z\IN e\\
&\mid&\mathbf{alias}(x=y\boxplus z)\SEQ e\mid\mathbf{alias}(x=\ast y)\SEQ e\mid\mathbf{assert}(\varphi)\SEQ e\\
\mathit{fd}&::=&f\mapsto(x_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,x_{\mathit{n}})\,e\\
D&::=&\{\mathit{fd_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\,...\,,\mathit{fd_{\mathit{n}}}\}\\
\mathit{P}&::=&\left\langle{D,e}\right\rangle\\
E&::=&[]\,|\,\LET x=E\IN e\\
\end{array}

C.2
Operational Semantics

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}x^{\prime}\not\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{R})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\LET x=n\IN e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R}\{x^{\prime}\mapsto n\},\mathit{H},[x^{\prime}/x]e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-LetInt
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}x^{\prime}\not\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{R})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\LET x=\mathbf{null}\IN e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{R\{x^{\prime}\mapsto\mathbf{null}\},H,[x^{\prime}/x]e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-LetNull
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}x^{\prime}\not\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{R})\quad\quad R(y),R(z)\in\mathbb{Z}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\LET x=y\mathop{-}z\IN e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{R\{x^{\prime}\mapsto R(y)-R(z)\},H,[x^{\prime}/x]e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-Minus
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\mathit{R}(x)\,\leq\,0\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\IFNP x\THEN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}\ELSE e_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},e_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-IfTrue
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\mathit{R}(x)\,>\,0\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\IFNP x\THEN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}\ELSE e_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},e_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-IfFalse
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}x^{\prime}\not\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{R})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\LET x=y\IN e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R}\{x^{\prime}\mapsto\mathit{R}(y)\},\mathit{H},[x^{\prime}/x]e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-LetVar
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}x^{\prime}\not\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{R})\quad\quad\mathit{R}(y)=(a,i)\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{H})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\LET x=\ast y\IN e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R}\{x^{\prime}\mapsto\mathit{H}(\mathit{R}(y))\},\mathit{H},[x^{\prime}/x]e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-Deref
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\mathit{R}(x)=(a,i)\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{H})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},x\WRITE y\SEQ e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H}\{(a,i)\hookleftarrow\mathit{R}(y)\},e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-Assign
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\mathit{R}(y)\,=\,pv\quad\quad x^{\prime}\not\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{R})\quad\quad R(z)\in\mathbb{Z}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\LET x=y\boxplus z\IN e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R}\{x^{\prime}\mapsto pv\boxplus\mathit{R}(z)\},\mathit{H},[x^{\prime}/x]e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-AddPtr
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}f\mapsto(x_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,x_{\mathit{n}})\,e\in D\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\LET x=f(y_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,y_{\mathit{n}})\IN e^{\prime}}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\LET x=[y_{{\mathrm{1}}}/x_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,y_{\mathit{n}}/x_{\mathit{n}}]e\IN e^{\prime}}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-Call
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}(a,0)\not\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{H})\quad\quad x^{\prime}\not\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{R})\quad\quad H^{\prime}=\mathit{H}\{(a,0),\ldots,(a,\mathit{R}(y)\,-\,1)\mapsto 0\}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\LET x=\ALLOC y:\mathbf{int}\TREF\IN e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R}\{x^{\prime}\mapsto(a,0)\},\mathit{H^{\prime}},[x^{\prime}/x]e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-MkArrayIntref
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}(a,0)\not\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{H})\quad\quad x^{\prime}\not\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{R})\\
\quad\quad\mathit{H^{\prime}}=\mathit{H}\{(a,0),\ldots,(a,\mathit{R}(y)\,-\,1)\mapsto\mathbf{null}\}\quad\quad R(y)\in\mathbb{Z}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\LET x=\ALLOC y:(\tau^{-}\TREF)\TREF\IN e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R}\{x^{\prime}\mapsto(a,0)\},\mathit{H^{\prime}},[x^{\prime}/x]e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-MkArrayNestedref
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\mathit{R}(y)\,=\,pv\quad\quad R(z)\in\mathbb{Z}\quad\quad\mathit{R}(x)=pv\boxplus\mathit{R}(z)\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\mathbf{alias}(x=y\boxplus z)\SEQ e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-AliasAddPtr
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\mathit{R}(y)\,=\,pv\quad\quad R(z)\in\mathbb{Z}\quad\quad\mathit{R}(x)\neq pv\boxplus\mathit{R}(z)\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\mathbf{alias}(x=y\boxplus z)\SEQ e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\mathbf{AliasFail}\end{array}}

(
Rs-AliasAddPtrFail
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\mathit{H}(\mathit{R}(y))\,=\,\mathit{R}(x)\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\mathbf{alias}(x=\ast y)\SEQ e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-AliasDeref
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}R(y)=\mathbf{null}\mbox{ or }\mathit{H}(\mathit{R}(y))\,\neq\,\mathit{R}(x)\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\mathbf{alias}(x=\ast y)\SEQ e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\mathbf{AliasFail}\end{array}}

(
Rs-AliasDerefFail
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\models[\mathit{R}]\,\varphi\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},\mathbf{assert}(\varphi)\SEQ e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-Assert
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R^{\prime}},\mathit{H^{\prime}},e^{\prime}}\right\rangle\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},E[e]}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R^{\prime}},\mathit{H^{\prime}},E[e^{\prime}]}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
Rs-Context
)

C.3
Type Well-Formedness

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\forall x\in\mathbf{FV}\,(\varphi)\backslash\{\nu\}.|\Gamma(x)|=\mathbf{int}\quad\quad\vdash\Gamma\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\varphi\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}

(
WF-Phi
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\varphi\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}

(
WF-Int
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models\textit{fml}({r=\mathbf{0}})\implies\textit{Empty}({\tau})\quad\quad\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash\tau\mbox{ ok}\quad\quad\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash r\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\Pi z.(\tau\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}

(
WF-Ref
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\forall x\in dom(\Gamma).\Gamma\vdash\Gamma(x)\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\vdash\Gamma\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}

(
WF-Env
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash r\mbox{ ok}\quad\quad q\in[0,1]\quad\quad\Gamma\vdash\varphi\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash(\varphi\produces q,r)\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}

(
WF-OwnPhi
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\vdash\Gamma\mbox{ ok}\quad\quad q\in[0,1]\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash(true\produces q)\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}

(
WF-OwnTrue
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\mathit{dom}(\Gamma)=\mathit{dom}(\Gamma^{\prime})\quad\quad\vdash\Gamma\mbox{ ok}\quad\quad\vdash\Gamma^{\prime}\mbox{ ok}\quad\quad\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\tau\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\vdash\left\langle{\Gamma}\right\rangle\ra\left\langle{\Gamma^{\prime}\mid\tau}\right\rangle\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}

(
WF-FunType
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\forall f\in dom(\Theta).\vdash\Theta(f)\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\vdash\Theta\mbox{ ok}\end{array}}

(
WF-FunEnv
)

C.4
Rules for Subtyping, Type Equivalence, and Type Addtion

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma,\nu\colon\mathbf{int}\models\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\implies\varphi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\}\leq\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\}\end{array}}

(
S-Int
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma,x\COL\mathbf{int}\models r_{1}\,\geq\,r_{2}\quad\quad\Gamma,x\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\Pi x.(\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{1}})\leq\Pi x.(\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{2}})\end{array}}

(
S-Ref
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\forall x\in\mathit{dom}(\Gamma^{\prime}).\Gamma\vdash\Gamma(x)\leq\Gamma^{\prime}(x)\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\leq\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
S-TyEnv
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma,x\COL\tau\leq\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau^{\prime}\quad\quad x\not\in\mathit{dom}(\Gamma)\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma,\tau\leq\Gamma^{\prime},\tau^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
S-Res
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\quad\quad\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\end{array}}

(
TEq-Sub
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\models\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\wedge\varphi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\iff\varphi_{{\mathrm{3}}}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\}+\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\}\approx\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{3}}}\}\end{array}}

(
TA-Int
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma,x\COL\mathbf{int}\models r_{{\mathrm{1}}}+r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,=\,r_{{\mathrm{3}}}\quad\quad\Gamma,x\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\Pi x.(\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{1}}}})+\Pi x.(\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\approx\Pi x.(\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{3}}}})\end{array}}

(
TA-Ref
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}\quad\quad\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{4}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{4}}}\end{array}}

(
TA-PlusPlus
)

C.5
Typing Rules for Expressions

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[x:\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}]\vdash x:\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\produces\Gamma\left[x\hookleftarrow\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\right]\end{array}}

(
T-Var
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma,x\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,n\}\vdash e:\tau\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau^{\prime})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash\LET x=n\IN e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-Int
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\models\textit{Empty}({\Pi z.(\tau\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})})\quad\quad\Theta\mid\Gamma,x\COL\Pi z.(\tau\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})\vdash e:\tau\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau^{\prime})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash\LET x=\mathbf{null}\IN e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-Null
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}][z:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi^{\prime}\}],x\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,y-z\}\vdash e:\tau\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau^{\prime})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}][z:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi^{\prime}\}]\vdash\LET x=y\mathop{-}z\IN e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-Minus
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[x\hookleftarrow\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\wedge\nu\,\leq\,0\}\right]\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\\
\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[x\hookleftarrow\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\wedge\nu\,>\,0\}\right]\vdash e_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[x:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}]\vdash\IFNP x\THEN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}\ELSE e_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-If
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta(f)=\left\langle{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,x_{\mathit{n}}\COL\tau_{\mathit{n}}}\right\rangle\ra\left\langle{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}\COL\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,x_{\mathit{n}}\COL\tau^{\prime}_{\mathit{n}}\mid\tau}\right\rangle\quad\quad\theta=[y_{{\mathrm{1}}}/x_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,y_{\mathit{n}}/x_{\mathit{n}}]\\
\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[y_{\mathit{i}}\hookleftarrow\theta\,\tau^{\prime}_{\mathit{i}}\right],x\COL\theta\,\tau\vdash e:\tau^{\prime}\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau^{\prime\prime})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[y_{\mathit{i}}:\theta\,\tau_{\mathit{i}}]\vdash\LET x=f(y_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,y_{\mathit{n}})\IN e:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-Call
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\quad\quad\Theta\mid\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau^{\prime}\vdash e_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau\produces(\Gamma^{\prime\prime},x\COL\tau^{\prime\prime})\quad\quad x\not\in(\Gamma^{\prime},\tau)\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash\LET x=e_{{\mathrm{0}}}\IN e_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-Let
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r\,=\,((0\,\leq\,z\wedge z\,\leq\,y-1)\produces 1)\\
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}],x\COL\Pi z.(\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid 0\,\leq\,z\wedge z\,\leq\,y-1\implies\nu\,=\,0\}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau^{\prime})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}]\vdash\LET x=\ALLOC y:\mathbf{int}\TREF\IN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-MkIntArray
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r\,=\,((0\,\leq\,z\wedge z\,\leq\,y-1)\produces 1)\quad\quad\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models\textit{Empty}({\tau^{\prime}})\\
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}],x\COL\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau^{\prime})\quad\quad|\tau^{\prime}|=\tau^{-}\TREF\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}]\vdash\LET x=\ALLOC y:(\tau^{-}\TREF)\TREF\IN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-MkNestedArray
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma,z\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\}\models r\,>\,\mathbf{0}\quad\quad\Gamma,z\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\}\vdash\tau^{\prime}+\tau_{x}\approx\tau_{y}\\
\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x},z\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{y}\approx(\tau^{\prime})^{=x}\quad\quad\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x},z\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,\neq\,0\}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{y}\approx\tau_{y}\\
\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[y\hookleftarrow\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}_{y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})\right],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau^{\prime\prime})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\Pi z.(\tau_{y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})]\vdash\LET x=\ast y\IN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-Deref
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma,z\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\}\models r\,=\,\mathbf{1}\quad\quad\Gamma\vdash\tau^{\prime}+\tau^{\prime}_{y}\approx\tau_{y}\\
\Gamma,z\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{\ast x}\approx(\tau^{\prime})^{=y}\quad\quad\Gamma,z\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,\neq\,0\}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{\ast x}\approx\tau_{\ast x}\\
\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[x\hookleftarrow\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}_{\ast x}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})\right]\left[y\hookleftarrow\tau^{\prime}_{y}\right]\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[x:\Pi z.(\tau_{\ast x}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})][y:\tau_{y}]\vdash x\WRITE y\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-Assign
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\vdash\Pi w.(\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{y_{{\mathrm{1}}}}})+\Pi w.[(w-z)/w](\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{x}})\approx\Pi w.(\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{y}})\\
\Theta\mid\Gamma[z:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}]\left[y\hookleftarrow\Pi w.(\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{y_{{\mathrm{1}}}}})\right],x\COL\Pi w.(\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{x}})\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau^{\prime})\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\Pi w.(\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{y}})][z:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}]\vdash\LET x=y\boxplus z\IN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-AddPtr
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\begin{array}[]{r}\Gamma\vdash(\Pi w^{\prime}.[(w^{\prime}-z)/w^{\prime}](\tau_{\ast x}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{x}})+\Pi w.(\tau_{\ast y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{y}}))\approx\\
(\Pi w^{\prime}.[(w^{\prime}-z)/w^{\prime}](\tau^{\prime}_{\ast x}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r^{\prime}}_{x}}))+\Pi w.(\tau^{\prime}_{\ast y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r^{\prime}}_{y}})\end{array}\\
\Theta\mid\Gamma[z:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}]\left[x\hookleftarrow\Pi w^{\prime}.(\tau^{\prime}_{\ast x}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r^{\prime}}_{x}})\right]\left[y\hookleftarrow\Pi w.(\tau^{\prime}_{\ast y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r^{\prime}}_{y}})\right]\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\\
\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[x:\Pi w^{\prime}.(\tau_{\ast x}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{x}})][y:\Pi w.(\tau_{\ast y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{y}})][z:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}]\vdash\mathbf{alias}(x=y\boxplus z)\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-AliasAddPtr
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\begin{array}[]{l}\Gamma,w\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\}\vdash\\
\qquad(\Pi z^{\prime}.(\tau_{\ast x}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{x}})+\Pi z.(\tau_{\ast\ast y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{\ast y}}))\approx(\Pi z^{\prime}.(\tau^{\prime}_{\ast x}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r^{\prime}}_{x}})+\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}_{\ast\ast y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r^{\prime}}_{\ast y}}))\end{array}\\
\Gamma,w\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,\neq\,0\}\vdash\Pi z.(\tau_{\ast\ast y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{\ast y}})\approx\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}_{\ast\ast y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r^{\prime}}_{\ast y}})\\
\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[x\hookleftarrow\Pi z^{\prime}.(\tau^{\prime}_{\ast x}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r^{\prime}}_{x}})\right]\left[y\hookleftarrow\Pi w.(\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}_{\ast\ast y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r^{\prime}}_{\ast y}})\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})\right]\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\\
\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma[x:\Pi z^{\prime}.(\tau_{\ast x}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{x}})][y:\Pi w.(\Pi z.(\tau_{\ast\ast y}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5pt{r}_{\ast y}})\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})]\vdash\mathbf{alias}(x=\ast y)\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-AliasDeref
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\models\varphi\quad\quad\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{assert}(\varphi)\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-Assert
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Gamma\leq\Gamma^{\prime}\quad\quad\Theta\mid\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime\prime}\quad\quad\Gamma^{\prime\prime},\tau\leq\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}\end{array}}

(
T-Sub
)

In
T-Int
, the body
e
of
\LET
is typed under the
pre-environment extended with
x\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,n\}
because
x
is
statically known to be
n
. Since the scope of
x
is

e
,
x\COL\tau^{\prime}
has to be dropped from the post-environment for

e
. If
x
appears in
\Gamma^{\prime}
, this rule cannot be applied
because
\Gamma^{\prime}
would be ill-formed. In such a case, subsumption
(
T-Sub
) has to be applied to weaken
\Gamma^{\prime}
not to mention

x
. The rules
T-Minus
and
T-Let
are similar. In

T-Minus
, the type of
z
represents that the value of
z

is exactly
x-y
. In
T-Let
,
\Gamma^{\prime}
stands for the
type environment after running
e_{{\mathrm{0}}}
.

C.6
Typing Rules for Functions and Programs

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta(f)=\left\langle{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}},\,...\,,x_{\mathit{n}}:\tau_{\mathit{n}}}\right\rangle\ra\left\langle{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\,...\,,x_{\mathit{n}}:\tau^{\prime}_{\mathit{n}}\mid\tau}\right\rangle\\
\Theta\mid x_{{\mathrm{1}}}\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,x_{\mathit{n}}\COL\tau_{\mathit{n}}\vdash e:\tau\produces x_{{\mathrm{1}}}\COL\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,x_{\mathit{n}}\COL\tau^{\prime}_{\mathit{n}}\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\vdash f\mapsto(x_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,x_{\mathit{n}})\,e\end{array}}

(
T-FunDef
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}dom(D)=dom(\Theta)\quad\quad\forall f\mapsto(x_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,x_{\mathit{n}})\,e\in D.\Theta\vdash f\mapsto(x_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,x_{\mathit{n}})\,e\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\vdash D\end{array}}

(
T-Funs
)

\displaystyle\frac{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\Theta\vdash D\quad\quad\vdash\Theta\mbox{ ok}\quad\quad\Theta\mid\bullet\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma\end{array}}{\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\vdash\left\langle{D,e}\right\rangle\end{array}}

(
T-Prog
)

Appendix D
Proofs

D.1
Configuration Typing

We introduce a typing judgment for configurations of the form

\vdash_{D}\left\langle{R,H,e}\right\rangle\COL\tau\Rightarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
, after a few auxiliary definitions.

Definition 1
.

We define the semantics
\left\llbracket{r}\right\rrbracket_{R}
of ownership expressions,
mappings
\mathbf{Own}
and
\mathbf{own}
, an eight-place relation

\mathbf{own}(H,R,v,\tau)\leq\mathbf{own}(H^{\prime},R^{\prime},v^{\prime},\tau^{\prime})
,
heap equivalence
\mathit{H_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\approx_{(a,i)}\mathit{H_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
, and
two relations
\mathbf{SAT}
and
\mathbf{SATv}
.

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\llbracket{\varphi\produces q,r}\right\rrbracket_{R}&\overset{\text{\tiny def}}{=}&\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}q&\mbox{if $\models[\mathit{R}]\,\varphi$}\\
\left\llbracket{r}\right\rrbracket_{R}&\mbox{otherwise}\end{array}\right.\\
\left\llbracket{true\produces q}\right\rrbracket_{R}&\overset{\text{\tiny def}}{=}&q\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbf{own}(H,R,v,\tau)&\overset{\text{\tiny def}}{=}&\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+l)\mapsto\llbracket[l/x]r\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}\\
+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/x]r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/x]\,\tau_{{\mathrm{0}}})\\
\hphantom{\emptyset}\text{\hskip 20.44434pt(if $\mathit{v}=(a,k)$ and $(a,k)\in\mathit{dom}(\mathit{H})$ and $\tau=\Pi x.(\tau_{{\mathrm{0}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})$)}\\
\emptyset\qquad\text{(otherwise)}\end{array}\right.\\
\mathbf{Own}(H,R,\Gamma)&\overset{\text{\tiny def}}{=}&\sum_{x\in\mathit{dom}(\Gamma)}\mathbf{own}(H,R,R(x),\Gamma(x))\\
\end{array}

\displaystyle\mathbf{own}(H,R,v,\tau)\leq\mathbf{own}(H^{\prime},R^{\prime},v^{\prime},\tau^{\prime})

\displaystyle\;\iff\begin{array}[]{l}dom(H)\subseteq dom(H^{\prime})\land dom(R)\subseteq dom(R^{\prime})\\
\land\forall(a,i)\in dom(\mathbf{own}(H,R,v,\tau)).\mathbf{own}(H,R,v,\tau)(a,i)\leq\mathbf{own}(H^{\prime},R^{\prime},v^{\prime},\tau^{\prime})(a,i)\end{array}

\displaystyle\mathit{H_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\approx_{(a,i)}\mathit{H_{{\mathrm{2}}}}

\displaystyle\;\iff dom(H_{1})=dom(H_{2})\land\forall(a^{\prime},i^{\prime})\in dom(H_{1})\setminus\{(a,i)\}.H_{1}(a^{\prime},i^{\prime})=H_{2}(a^{\prime},i^{\prime})

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbf{SAT}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\Gamma)&\iff&\mathbf{SATv}(H,R,R(x),\Gamma(x))\mbox{ for any $x\in\mathit{dom}(\Gamma)$}\\
\mathbf{SATv}(H,R,n,\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\})&\iff&\models[R][n/\nu]\varphi\\
\mathbf{SATv}(H,R,\mathbf{null},\Pi z.(\tau\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr}))&\iff&\forall j.\left\llbracket{r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}\{z\mapsto j\}}=0\\
\mathbf{SATv}(H,R,(a,k),\Pi z.(\tau\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr}))&\iff&\begin{array}[t]{l}\forall j.\left\llbracket{r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}\{z\mapsto j\}}>0\implies\\
(a,k+j)\in\mathit{dom}(H)\\
\mbox{ and }\mathbf{SATv}(H,R,H(a,k+j),[j/z]\tau)\\
\end{array}\\
\end{array}

Definition 2

(Consistency of ownership)
.

We write
\mathbf{ConOwn}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\Gamma)
if
\mathbf{Own}(H,R,\Gamma)(a,k)\leq 1
for any
(a,k)\in\mathit{dom}(H)
.

Definition 3

(Configuration typing)
.

We write
\vdash_{D}\left\langle{R,H,e}\right\rangle\COL\tau\Rightarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
if
there exist
\Theta
and
\Gamma
that satisfy
(1)
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
,
(2)
\Theta\vdash D
,
(3)
\mathbf{ConOwn}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\Gamma)
, and
(4)
\mathbf{SAT}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\Gamma)
.

D.2
Proof of Theorem
3.1

We recall Theorem
3.1
.

See
3.1

This theorem easily follows from the following lemmas.

Lemma 4
.

If
\vdash\left\langle{D,e}\right\rangle
, then
\vdash_{D}\left\langle{\emptyset,\emptyset,e}\right\rangle\COL\tau\Rightarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
for some
\tau
and
\Gamma^{\prime}
.

Proof.

From
\vdash\left\langle{D,e}\right\rangle
, we have
\Theta\vdash D
and

\Theta\mid\bullet\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
for
some
\Theta,\tau,\Gamma^{\prime}
. It is enough to show

\mathbf{ConOwn}(\emptyset,\emptyset,\bullet)
and

\mathbf{SAT}(\emptyset,\emptyset,\bullet)
, which are obvious from the
definitions of
\mathbf{ConOwn}
and
\mathbf{SAT}
.
∎

Lemma 5

(Preservation)
.

If
\vdash_{D}\left\langle{R,H,e}\right\rangle\COL\tau\Rightarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
and

\left\langle{\mathit{R},\mathit{H},e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}\left\langle{\mathit{R^{\prime}},\mathit{H^{\prime}},e^{\prime}}\right\rangle
, then

\vdash_{D}\left\langle{R^{\prime},H^{\prime},e^{\prime}}\right\rangle\COL\tau\Rightarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
.

Proof.

See Section
D.2.1
.
∎

Lemma 6

(Progress)
.

If
\vdash_{D}\left\langle{H,R,e}\right\rangle\COL\tau\Rightarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
, then (1) there exists
C^{\prime}
such that
\left\langle{H,R,e}\right\rangle\longrightarrow_{D}C^{\prime}
, or
(2)
e=x
for some variable
x
.

Proof.

See Section
D.2.2
.
∎

D.2.1
Proof of
\cref
lem:preservation

Lemma 7

(Weakening)
.

If
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e\COL\tau\Rightarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
and

x
is fresh, then

\Theta\mid\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e\COL\tau\Rightarrow\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}

Proof.

By the induction on the derivation of
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
with case analysis on the last typing rule used.

T-Var
and
T-Null
are trivial.

Case

T-Int
:
We show that
\Theta\mid\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\LET y=n\IN e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
if
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash\LET y=n\IN e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.
Because the last rule used to derive
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash\LET y=n\IN e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
is
T-Int
, the following hold:

1.

\Theta\mid\Gamma,y\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,n\}\vdash e:\tau\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},y\COL\tau^{\prime})
.

for some
\tau^{\prime}
.
By I.H. and
1
,
\Theta\mid\Gamma,y\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,n\},x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e:\tau\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},y\COL\tau^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x})
holds.
From the rule
T-Int
,
\Theta\mid\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\LET y=n\IN e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
holds.

Case

T-LetExp
,
T-Let
,
T-AddPtr
and
T-Minus
:
Similar to the case of
T-Int
.

Case

T-If
:
We show that
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\IFNP y\THEN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}\ELSE e_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}

if
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\IFNP y\THEN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}\ELSE e_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.
Because the last rule used to derive
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\IFNP y\THEN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}\ELSE e_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
is
T-If
,
the following hold:

1.

\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[y\hookleftarrow\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\wedge\nu\,\leq\,0\}\right]\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}

2.

\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[y\hookleftarrow\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\wedge\nu\,>\,0\}\right]\vdash e_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.

By I.H.,
\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[y\hookleftarrow\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\wedge\nu\,\leq\,0\}\right],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}

and

\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[y\hookleftarrow\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\wedge\nu\,>\,0\}\right],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
hold.
From the rule
T-If
,
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\IFNP y\THEN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}\ELSE e_{{\mathrm{1}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
holds.

Case

T-Call
:
We show that
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y_{\mathit{i}}:\theta\,\tau_{\mathit{i}}],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\LET z=f(y_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,y_{\mathit{n}})\IN e:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}

if
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y_{\mathit{i}}:\theta\,\tau_{\mathit{i}}]\vdash\LET z=f(y_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,y_{\mathit{n}})\IN e:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.
Because the last rule used to derive
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y_{\mathit{i}}:\theta\,\tau_{\mathit{i}}]\vdash\LET z=f(y_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,y_{\mathit{n}})\IN e:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
is
T-Call
,
the following hold:

1.

\Theta(f)=\left\langle{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,x_{\mathit{n}}\COL\tau_{\mathit{n}}}\right\rangle\ra\left\langle{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}\COL\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,x_{\mathit{n}}\COL\tau^{\prime}_{\mathit{n}}\mid\tau}\right\rangle

2.

\theta=[y_{{\mathrm{1}}}/x_{{\mathrm{1}}}]\cdots[y_{\mathit{n}}/x_{\mathit{n}}]

3.

\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[y_{\mathit{i}}\hookleftarrow\theta\,\tau^{\prime}_{\mathit{i}}\right],z\COL\theta\,\tau\vdash e:\tau^{\prime}\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},z\COL\tau^{\prime\prime})
.

By I.H. and
3
,
\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[y_{\mathit{i}}\hookleftarrow\theta\,\tau^{\prime}_{\mathit{i}}\right],z\COL\theta\,\tau,x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e:\tau^{\prime}\produces(\Gamma^{\prime},z\COL\tau^{\prime\prime},x\COL\tau_{x})
holds.
From the rule
T-Call
,
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y_{\mathit{i}}:\theta\,\tau_{\mathit{i}}],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\LET z=f(y_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ldots,y_{\mathit{n}})\IN e:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
.

Case

T-Assert
:
We show that
\Theta\mid\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\mathbf{assert}(\varphi)\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
if
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{assert}(\varphi)\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.
Because the last rule used to derive
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{assert}(\varphi)\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
is
T-Assert
,
the following hold:

1.

\models\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\produces\varphi

2.

\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.

By I.H. and
2
,
\Theta\mid\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
holds.
Since
x
is fresh,
the update from
\Gamma
to
\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}
does not affect the result and the validity of
\varphi
,
that is,

3.

\models\llbracket\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}\rrbracket\produces\varphi\iff\models\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\produces\varphi

From the rule
T-Assert
,
\Theta\mid\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\mathbf{assert}(\varphi)\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
.

Case

T-AliasAddPtr
and
T-AliasDeref
:
Similar to the case of
T-Assert
.

Case

T-Assign
:
We show that
\Theta\mid\Gamma[z:\Pi w.(\tau_{\ast z}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})][y:\tau_{y}],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash z\WRITE y\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}

if
\Theta\mid\Gamma[z:\Pi w.(\tau_{\ast z}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})][y:\tau_{y}]\vdash z\WRITE y\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.
Because the last rule used to derive
\Theta\mid\Gamma[z:\Pi w.(\tau_{\ast z}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})][y:\tau_{y}]\vdash z\WRITE y\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
is
T-Assign
,
the following hold:

1.

\Gamma,w\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{\ast z}\approx(\tau^{\prime})^{=y}

2.

\Gamma,w\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,\neq\,0\}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{\ast z}\approx\tau_{\ast z}

3.

\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[z\hookleftarrow\Pi w.(\tau^{\prime}_{\ast z}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})\right]\left[y\hookleftarrow\tau^{\prime}_{y}\right]\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}

4.

\Gamma,w\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\}\models r\,=\,\mathbf{1}

5.

\Gamma\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{y}+\tau^{\prime}\approx\tau_{y}
.

for some
\tau^{\prime}
.
By I.H. and
3
,
\Theta\mid\Gamma\left[z\hookleftarrow\Pi w.(\tau^{\prime}_{\ast z}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})\right]\left[y\hookleftarrow\tau^{\prime}_{y}\right],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
holds.
Since
x
is fresh, the update from
\Gamma
to
\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}
does not affect the equality of ownership terms or the validity of propositions,
that is,

6.

\Gamma,w\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{\ast z}\approx(\tau^{\prime})^{=y}\iff\Gamma,w\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\},x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{\ast z}\approx(\tau^{\prime})^{=y}

7.

\Gamma,w\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,\neq\,0\}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{\ast z}\approx\tau_{\ast z}\iff\Gamma,w\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,\neq\,0\},x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{\ast z}\approx\tau_{\ast z}

8.

\Gamma,w\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\}\models r\,=\,\mathbf{1}\iff\Gamma,w\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\nu\,=\,0\},x\COL\tau_{x}\models r\,=\,\mathbf{1}

9.

\Gamma\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{y}+\tau^{\prime}\approx\tau_{y}\iff\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{y}+\tau^{\prime}\approx\tau_{y}
.

From the rule T-Assign,
\Theta\mid\Gamma[z:\Pi w.(\tau_{\ast z}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})][y:\tau_{y}],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash z\WRITE y\SEQ e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
holds.

Case

T-Deref
:
Similar to the case of
T-Assign
.

Case

T-MkIntArray
:
We show that
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\LET z=\ALLOC y:\mathbf{int}\TREF\IN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}

if
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}]\vdash\LET z=\ALLOC y:\mathbf{int}\TREF\IN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.
Because the last rule used to derive
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}]\vdash\LET z=\ALLOC y:\mathbf{int}\TREF\IN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
is
T-MkIntArray
,
the following hold:

1.

\Gamma,w\COL\mathbf{int}\models r\,=\,((0\,\leq\,w\wedge w\,\leq\,y-1)\produces 1,\mathbf{0})

2.

\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}],z\COL\Pi w.(\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid 0\,\leq\,w\wedge w\,\leq\,y-1\implies\nu\,=\,0\}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},z\COL\tau^{\prime}

By I.H. and
2
,

\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}],z\COL\Pi w.(\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid 0\,\leq\,w\wedge w\,\leq\,y-1\implies\nu\,=\,0\}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr}),x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},z\COL\tau^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
holds.
Because
x
is fresh, the update from
\Gamma
to
\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}
does not affect ownership term, that is,

\Gamma,w\COL\mathbf{int}\models r\,=\,((0\,\leq\,w\wedge w\,\leq\,y-1)\produces 1,\mathbf{0})\iff\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x},w\COL\mathbf{int}\models r\,=\,((0\,\leq\,w\wedge w\,\leq\,y-1)\produces 1,\mathbf{0}).

From the rule
T-MkIntArray
,
\Theta\mid\Gamma[y:\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}],x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash\LET z=\ALLOC y:\mathbf{int}\TREF\IN e_{{\mathrm{0}}}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
.

Case

T-MkNestedArray
:
Similar to the case of
T-MkIntArray
.

Case

T-Sub
:
We show that
\Theta\mid\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}

if
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.
Because the last rule used to derive
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
is
T-Sub
,
the following hold:

1.

\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash e:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}

2.

\Gamma\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}

3.

\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\tau^{\prime}\leq\Gamma^{\prime},\tau

By I.H. and
1
,

\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},x\COL\tau_{x}
holds.
From the the definition of
\leq
and
3
,

\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},x\COL\tau_{x},\tau^{\prime}\leq\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x},\tau
holds.
Thanks to the rule
T-Sub
,
\Theta\mid\Gamma,x\COL\tau_{x}\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},x\COL\tau_{x}
.
∎

Lemma 8
.

The relations
\Gamma\vdash\tau\leq\tau^{\prime}
and
\Gamma\leq\Gamma^{\prime}
are reflexive and transitive.

Proof.

We will prove in the following order:

1.

the reflexivity of
\Gamma\vdash\tau\leq\tau^{\prime}

2.

the transitivity of
\Gamma\vdash\tau\leq\tau^{\prime}

3.

the reflexivity of
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}

4.

the transitivity of
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}

•

The proof of the reflexivity of
\Gamma\vdash\tau\leq\tau^{\prime}

By induction on the derivation of
\Gamma\vdash\tau\leq\tau
.
The base case is when
\tau=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}
.
Since
\varphi\implies\varphi
is tautology,
\models\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\produces(\varphi\implies\varphi)
always holds.
Thus we get
\Gamma\vdash\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}\leq\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}
by
S-Int
.

The inductive case is the case where
\tau=\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})
with
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash\tau^{\prime}\leq\tau^{\prime}
.
By
S-Ref
, our goal is to show that
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r\,\geq\,r
and
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash\tau^{\prime}\leq\tau^{\prime}
.
The former is trivial, and the latter follows from the I.H.

•

the proof of transitivity of
\tau\leq\tau^{\prime}

By induction on the derivation of
\tau\leq\tau^{\prime}
, that is,

\forall

\Gamma
,
\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,
\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
,
\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
. if
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
, then
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
.
The base case is when
\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\}
,
\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\}
,
\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{3}}}\}

with
\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
.
By definition of
\leq
,
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
means
\models\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\produces(\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\implies\varphi_{{\mathrm{2}}})
and
\models\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\produces(\varphi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\implies\varphi_{{\mathrm{3}}})
.
By transitivity of logical relation
\implies
,
\models\llbracket\Gamma\rrbracket\produces(\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\implies\varphi_{{\mathrm{3}}})
holds.
Thus we get
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
by
S-Int
.

The inductive case is the case where

\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
,

\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}=\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
,

\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}=\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{3}}}})

with

\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
,

\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,r_{{\mathrm{2}}}
,

\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}
, and

\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,\geq\,r_{{\mathrm{3}}}
.
By I.H.,
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}
.
Also,
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,r_{{\mathrm{3}}}
holds.
Finally,
S-Ref
shows
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
.

•

the proof of reflexivity of
\Gamma\leq\Gamma^{\prime}

It easily follows from the reflexivity of
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

•

the proof of transitivity of
\Gamma\leq\Gamma^{\prime}

It easily follows from the transitivity of
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
∎

Lemma 9
.

If
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash E[e]\COL\tau\Rightarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
, then
there exist
\Gamma_{1},\tau^{\prime}
and fresh
z
such that

1.

\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e\COL\tau^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Gamma_{1}
and

2.

\Theta\mid\Gamma_{1},z\COL\tau^{\prime}\vdash E[z]\COL\tau\Rightarrow\Gamma^{\prime}
.

Proof.

By induction on
E
.
The base case is when
E=[]
and
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
holds.
Choose
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\Gamma^{\prime}
,
\tau^{\prime}=\tau
, and let
z
be a fresh variable.
Condition 1,
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e\COL\tau^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Gamma_{1}
is precisely the premise itself.
To show Condition 2, using the
T-Sub
rule, we show the following three points.

3.

\Gamma,z\COL\tau\leq\Gamma,z\COL\tau

4.

\Theta\mid\Gamma^{\prime},z\COL\tau\vdash z:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime},z\COL\tau^{\prime\prime}

where
\tau
and
\tau^{\prime\prime}
have the same simple type and
\Gamma\models
Empty
(
\tau^{\prime\prime}
) holds

5.

(\Gamma^{\prime},z\COL\tau),\tau\leq\Gamma^{\prime},\tau
.

3
is shown by Lemma
8
.

4
is derived by the rule T-Var.
Here, we used
\Gamma,z\COL\tau\vdash\tau+\tau^{\prime\prime}\approx\tau
.
It is easy to show that
\Gamma\models\tau+\tau^{\prime\prime}\approx\tau
for any
\tau
and
\tau^{\prime\prime}
such that

|\tau|=|\tau^{\prime\prime}|
and
\Gamma\models\textit{Empty}({\tau^{\prime\prime}})
hold.

5
follows from definition of the relation
\leq
between
\Gamma^{\prime}
.

The inductive case is the case where
E=\LET x=E^{\prime}\IN e^{\prime}

with
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash\LET x=E^{\prime}[e]\IN e^{\prime}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
and
if
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash E^{\prime}[e]:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
then there exists
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and fresh
z_{2}
such that

\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e:\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z_{{\mathrm{2}}}\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash E^{\prime}[z]:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.
We must show the following two points. There exist
\Gamma_{1},\tau^{\prime}
and fresh
z
such that

6.

\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}

7.

\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},z\COL\tau^{\prime}\vdash E[z]:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}

Given that the judgment
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash\LET x=E^{\prime}[e]\IN e^{\prime}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
holds,
and from the conclusive form of the inference rules,
this judgment must be derived by an application of
T-LetExp
(following some derivation) and
zero or more applications of
T-Sub
.
Therefore, there exists
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}},\tau_{{\mathrm{4}}}
such that
\Gamma\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{4}}}\leq\tau
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\leq\Gamma^{\prime}
and

8.

\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash E^{\prime}[e]:\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}

9.

\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},x\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash e^{\prime}:\tau_{{\mathrm{4}}}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}
.

By I.H. and
8
, there exists
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}},\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}
and fresh
z_{5}
such that

10.

\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash e:\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}

11.

\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}},z_{{\mathrm{5}}}\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash E^{\prime}[z_{{\mathrm{5}}}]:\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}
.

For this
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}
,
\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}
, and
z_{{\mathrm{5}}}
, we show the following,
which is the conclusion of the proposition.

12.

\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash e:\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}

13.

\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}},z_{{\mathrm{5}}}\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash E^{\prime}[z_{{\mathrm{5}}}]:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.

From
\Gamma\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
,
10
,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}},\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}},\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}
, and the rule
T-Sub
,
we have
12
.
By
9
,
11
and the rule
T-LetExp
,
we derive
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}},z_{{\mathrm{5}}}\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash\LET x=E^{\prime}[z_{{\mathrm{5}}}]\IN e^{\prime}:\tau_{{\mathrm{4}}}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}
for fresh
x
.
Now, apply
T-Sub
to this result.
The premises are
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}},z_{{\mathrm{5}}}\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}},z_{{\mathrm{5}}}\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}
(reflexivity by Lemma
8
),

\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\leq\Gamma^{\prime}
, and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{4}}}\leq\tau
.
Thus,
T-Sub
yields
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}},z_{{\mathrm{5}}}\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{5}}}\vdash\LET x=E^{\prime}[z_{{\mathrm{5}}}]\IN e^{\prime}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.

∎

Lemma 10

(Inversion for expression typing)
.

1.

If
\Theta\mid\Gamma\vdash x:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
, then there exist

\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,
\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}},\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, and
\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}

such that
(1)
\Gamma\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}
,
(2)
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}(x)=\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
,
(3)
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}

Lemma 11
.

If
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
, then
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
.

Lemma 12
.

If
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and

\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau\vdash E[z]:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}

and
z\not\in\mathbf{FV}(E)
, then

\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash E[e]:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.

Proof.

By induction on
E
.
The base case is when
E=[]
and
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau\vdash z:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
hold.
Given that the judgment
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau\vdash z:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
holds,
and from the conclusive form of the inference rules,
this judgment must be derived by an application of
T-Var
(following some derivation) and
zero or more applications of
T-Sub
.
Therefore, there exists
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}},\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, and
\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}

such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}

and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\Gamma^{\prime},\tau^{\prime}

and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}(z)=\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}

and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}

and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\left[z\hookleftarrow\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\right]
.

To derive
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash e:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
by applying
T-Sub
to
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, we prove the following formula.

\displaystyle\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}

(4)

\displaystyle\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}

(5)

\displaystyle\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\tau\leq\Gamma^{\prime},\tau^{\prime}.

(6)

(
4
) is shown by Lemma
8
;
(
5
) is an assumption.
Now we check (
6
). By rule
S-TyEnv
.

\displaystyle\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\tau\leq\tau^{\prime}

(7)

\displaystyle\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\Gamma^{\prime}

(8)

Because
\tau=(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau)(z)
,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau\vdash(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau)(z)\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}(z)
(by
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}
),

\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}
(by
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\left[z\hookleftarrow\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\right]
,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}(z)=\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and Lemma
11
) and
Lemma
8
, we get (
7
).
Since
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}
(by
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and z is fresh),
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\leq\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}
(by Lemma
11
) and

\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}\leq\Gamma^{\prime}
(by
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}},\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\leq\Gamma^{\prime},\tau^{\prime}
), we have
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\leq\Gamma
.

The inductive case is the case where
E=\LET x=E^{\prime}\IN e^{\prime}

and
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau\vdash\LET x=E^{\prime}[z]\IN e^{\prime}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
holds
with
if
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash e:\tau\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau\vdash E^{\prime}[z]:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
for fresh
z^{\prime}
, then
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash E^{\prime}[e]:\tau^{\prime}\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
.
Given that the judgment
\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},z\COL\tau\vdash\LET x=E^{\prime}[z]\IN e^{\prime}:\tau\produces\Gamma^{\prime}
holds,
and from the conclusive form of the inference rules,
this judgment must be derived by an application of
T-LetExp
(following some derivation) and
zero or more applications of
T-Sub
.
Therefore, there exists
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}
,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}
,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}
,
\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
,
\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}

such that

,

and

\displaystyle x\notin dom(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}})\cup\mathbf{FV}\,(\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}})

(9)

\displaystyle\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash E^{\prime}[z]:\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}}

(10)

\displaystyle\Theta\mid\Gamma_{{\mathrm{5}}},x\COL\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}\vdash e^{\prime}:\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\produces\Gamma_{{\mathrm{4}}}.

,

, (

), and I.H., we have

,

(15)

Lemma 13
.

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto v^{\prime}\},v,\tau)=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau)
if
x
is fresh.

Proof.

By induction on
\tau
.
The base case is
\tau=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}
, which is trivial because

\displaystyle\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto v^{\prime}\},v,\tau)

\displaystyle\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau)

\displaystyle=\emptyset

The induction case is
\tau=\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})
for some
\tau^{\prime}
and
r
.
The case where
v=\mathbf{null}
or
v\notin dom(H)
is straightforward.
Suppose
v=(a,k)\in dom(H)
, where
(a,k)
is an address such that

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto v^{\prime}\},v^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime})=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v^{\prime\prime},\tau^{\prime})
.
Then,

\displaystyle\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto v^{\prime}\},(a,k),\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr}))

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+l)\mapsto\llbracket[l/z]r\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto v^{\prime}\}}\}+

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto v^{\prime}\},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime})

(by definition of
\mathbf{own}
)

Here, since
r
consists only of variables from
\Gamma
and array indices, and is independent of
x
,
it follows that

\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+l)\mapsto\llbracket[l/z]r\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto v^{\prime}\}}\}=\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+l)\mapsto\llbracket[l/z]r\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}.

(16)

By I.H.

\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto v^{\prime}\},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime})=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime}).

(17)

So,

\displaystyle=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto v^{\prime}\},(a,k),\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr}))

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+l)\mapsto\llbracket[l/z]r\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto v^{\prime}\}}\}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto v^{\prime}\},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+l)\mapsto\llbracket[l/z]r\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime})

(by (
16
) and (
17
))

\displaystyle=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},(a,k),\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr}))

∎

Lemma 14
.

1.

\mathbf{ConOwn}(H,R\left\{{x\mapsto n}\right\},(\Gamma,x\COL\left\{{\nu\COL\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi}\right\}))
if
\mathbf{ConOwn}(H,R,\Gamma)
and
x\notin dom(\Gamma)\cup dom(R)
.

2.

\mathbf{ConOwn}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\Gamma\left[x\hookleftarrow\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\}\right])

if
\mathbf{ConOwn}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\Gamma)
and
\Gamma(x)=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\}
.

Proof.

The proof of
1

Because of
x\notin dom(\Gamma)
and Lemma
13
, for any
y\in dom(\Gamma)
,

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{R}(y),\Gamma(y))=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto n\},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto n\}(y),(\Gamma,x\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\})(y)).

(18)

By definition of
\mathbf{own}

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto n\},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto n\}(x),(\Gamma,x\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\})(x))=\emptyset.

(19)

From (
18
) and (
19
),
\mathbf{Own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\Gamma)=\mathbf{Own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R}\{x\mapsto n\},(\Gamma,x\COL\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}))
, that is,

\mathbf{ConOwn}(H,R\left\{{x\mapsto n}\right\},(\Gamma,x\COL\left\{{\nu\COL\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi}\right\}))
holds if
\mathbf{ConOwn}(H,R,\Gamma)
.

The proof of
2

Because refinement does not affect
\mathbf{own}
,
for any
y\in dom(\Gamma\setminus\{x\})
,

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{R}(y),\Gamma\left[x\hookleftarrow\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\}\right](y))=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{R}(y),\Gamma(y)).

(20)

Furthermore,

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{R}(x),\Gamma\left[x\hookleftarrow\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\}\right](x))=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{R}(x),\Gamma(x))=\emptyset.

(21)

From (
20
) and (
21
),

\mathbf{Own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\Gamma\left[x\hookleftarrow\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\}\right])=\mathbf{Own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\Gamma)
, that is,

\mathbf{ConOwn}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\Gamma\left[x\hookleftarrow\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\}\right])

if
\mathbf{ConOwn}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\Gamma)
.
∎

Lemma 15
.

If
\Gamma\models r_{{\mathrm{1}}}+r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,=\,r_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and

\models[\mathit{R}]\,\textit{fml}({\Gamma})
, then

\llbracket r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}+\llbracket r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}=\llbracket r_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}
.

Proof.

\displaystyle\Gamma\models r_{{\mathrm{1}}}+r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,=\,r_{{\mathrm{3}}}

\displaystyle\iff\models\textit{fml}({\Gamma})\implies r_{{\mathrm{1}}}+r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\,=\,r_{{\mathrm{3}}}

(by definition)

\displaystyle\implies\models[\mathit{R}]\,\textit{fml}({\Gamma})\implies\llbracket r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}+\llbracket r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\,=\,\llbracket r_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}

\displaystyle\implies\llbracket r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}+\llbracket r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\,=\,\llbracket r_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}

∎

Lemma 16
.

\forall j\in\mathbb{Z}.\llbracket[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\,\geq\,\llbracket[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}
holds if

\models[\mathit{R}]\,\textit{fml}({\Gamma})
and
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,r_{2}
.

Proof.

\displaystyle\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,r_{{\mathrm{2}}}

\displaystyle\models\textit{fml}({\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}})\implies r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,r_{{\mathrm{2}}}

\displaystyle\models\textit{fml}({\Gamma})\implies r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,r_{{\mathrm{2}}}

(by
\textit{fml}_{z}({\mathbf{int}})=\top
)

\displaystyle\implies\models[\mathit{R}]\,(\textit{fml}({\Gamma})\implies r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,r_{{\mathrm{2}}})

\displaystyle\iff\models[\mathit{R}]\,\textit{fml}({\Gamma})\implies[\mathit{R}]\,(r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,r_{{\mathrm{2}}})

\displaystyle\iff[\mathit{R}]\,(r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,r_{{\mathrm{2}}})

(by
\models[\mathit{R}]\,\textit{fml}({\Gamma})
)

\displaystyle\iff[\mathit{R}]\,r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,[\mathit{R}]r_{{\mathrm{2}}}

The above logical formula transformation holds for an arbitrary
z
.
Since
z
represents any integer, we may substitute
j
for it, yielding:

\forall j\in\mathbb{Z}.\llbracket[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\,\geq\,\llbracket[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}
.
∎

Lemma 17
.

If
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash\tau\leq\tau^{\prime}
, then
\Gamma\vdash[j/z]\,\tau\leq[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime}
.

Proof.

Since
\tau\leq\tau^{\prime}
holds for any integer
z
,
[j/z]\,\tau\leq[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime}
(by substituting
j
for
z
) also holds.
∎

Lemma 18
.

\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,=\,r_{{\mathrm{2}}}
hold if
\Gamma\vdash\Pi z.(\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\approx\Pi z.(\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
.

Proof.

By definition of
\approx
.
∎

Lemma 19
.

\forall j\in\mathbb{Z}.\llbracket[j/z]r_{1}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}=\llbracket[j/z]r_{2}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}
holds if

\models[\mathit{R}]\,\textit{fml}({\Gamma})
and
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,=\,r_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

This follows immediately from Lemma
16
.
∎

Lemma 20
.

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau^{\prime})\leq\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau)
holds if
\Gamma\vdash\tau\leq\tau^{\prime}
and
\models[\mathit{R}]\,\textit{fml}({\Gamma})
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\tau\leq\tau^{\prime}
.
The base case is
\tau=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}
and

\tau^{\prime}=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi^{\prime}\}
, which is trivial because
\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau)=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau^{\prime})=\emptyset
.

The induction case is
\tau=\Pi z.(\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
and
\tau^{\prime}=\Pi z.(\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{2}}}})

with
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,r_{{\mathrm{2}}}

,
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and if
\Gamma\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
, then
\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v^{\prime},\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}})\geq\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v^{\prime},\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}})
holds.
When
v=\mathbf{null}
and
v\notin dom(H)
, this is straightforward.
When
v=(a,k)\in dom(H)
, where
(a,k)
is address,

\displaystyle\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},(a,k),\Pi z.(\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{1}}}}))

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+j)\mapsto\llbracket[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}})

(by definition of
\mathbf{own}
)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+j)\mapsto\llbracket[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}})

(by Lemma
17
,
(\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int})\vdash\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}\leq\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and I.H. )

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+j)\mapsto\llbracket[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}})

(by Lemma
16
and
\Gamma,z\COL\mathbf{int}\models r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\,\geq\,r_{{\mathrm{2}}}
)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},(a,k),\Pi z.(\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{2}}}}))

(by definition of
\mathbf{own}
)

∎

Lemma 21
.

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau)=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau^{\prime})

\Gamma\vdash\tau\approx\tau^{\prime}

\models[\mathit{R}]\,\textit{fml}({\Gamma})
.

Lemma 22
.

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau)=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau^{=x})
holds.

Proof.

We consider two cases.
The first case is
\tau=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi\}
, which is trivial because

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau)=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau^{=x})=\emptyset

The second case is
\tau=\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})
.
By definition of
\tau^{=x}
, we have
(\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr}))^{=x}=\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})
.
Therefore,
\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,(\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr}))^{=x})=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr}))
.
In either case, this lemma holds.
∎

Lemma 23
.

If
\Gamma\models\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
and
\models[\mathit{R}]\,\textit{fml}({\Gamma})
, then

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}})=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}})
.

Proof.

By induction of
\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
.
The base case is
\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{3}}}\}
.
From
\Gamma\models\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
,

\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}\}
and
\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\{\nu:\mathbf{int}\mid\varphi_{{\mathrm{2}}}\}
for some
\varphi_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\varphi_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
By definition,
\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}})=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}})=\emptyset
.
Thus,
\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}})=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}})
.

The inductive case is the case where
\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}=\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{3}}}})

with
\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{1}}}})
,
\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}=\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
,
\Gamma\models\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}}\approx\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}}
,

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})

for some
\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},r_{1},r_{2}
.
The case where
v=\mathbf{null}
or
v\notin dom(H)
is trivial.
Otherwise,
v
is the address
(a,k)\in dom(H)
, satisfying

\displaystyle\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},(a,k),\tau_{{\mathrm{1}}})+\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},(a,k),\tau_{{\mathrm{2}}})

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+j)\mapsto\llbracket[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})\\
&\quad+\sum_{l\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+j)\mapsto\llbracket[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})\end{aligned}

(by definition of
\mathbf{own}
)

\displaystyle=\begin{aligned} &\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+j)\mapsto\llbracket[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}\\
&\quad+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}})\\
&+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}})\end{aligned}

(by Lemma
15
)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a,k+j)\mapsto\llbracket[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{3}}}\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r_{{\mathrm{3}}}}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a,k+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{3}}})

(by I.H.)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},(a,k),\tau_{{\mathrm{3}}})

(by definition of
\mathbf{own}
)

∎

Lemma 24
.

\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau)(a,i)=0
if
(a,i)\in dom(\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau))
,
\models[\mathit{R}]\,\textit{fml}({\Gamma})
and
\Gamma\models\textit{Empty}({\tau})
.

Proof.

We consider only the case where
v=(a^{\prime},i^{\prime})
and
(a^{\prime},i^{\prime})\in dom(H)
and
\tau=\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})
for some
a^{\prime},i^{\prime},\tau^{\prime},r
.
The other cases are trivial because
\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},v,\tau)=\emptyset

\displaystyle\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},(a^{\prime},i^{\prime}),\tau)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a^{\prime},i^{\prime}+j)\mapsto\llbracket[j/z]r\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}+\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a^{\prime},i^{\prime}+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime})

(By definition of
\mathbf{own}
)

Since
\Gamma\models\textit{Empty}({\Pi z.(\tau^{\prime}\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr})})
,

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\{(a^{\prime},i^{\prime}+j)\mapsto\llbracket[j/z]r\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}\}(a,i)=0.

(22)

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathbb{Z}\land\left\llbracket{[j/z]r}\right\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0}\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},\mathit{H}((a^{\prime},i^{\prime}+j)),[j/z]\,\tau^{\prime})=\emptyset

(23)

This lemma is proven by Equation
22
and
23
.
∎

Lemma 25
.

If
\mathbf{own}(\mathit{H},\mathit{R},(a^{\prime},i^{\prime}),\Pi z.(\tau\TREF^{\hskip 0.5ptr}))(a,i)=0
and
\llbracket[j/z]r\rrbracket_{\mathit{R}}>0
, then
(a,i)\neq(a^{\prime},i^{\prime}+j)
.

\mathbf{own}
.
